{"forms":[{"form":"scuba sets","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The set of equipment required by a scuba diver to be at ease when submerged for extended periods of time. Modern equipment sets include tanks containing mixes of nitrogen in varied ratios with oxygen, a buoyancy control device, a first- and second-stage regulator (to allow the regulated flow of gas from the tank into the user's respiratory system), a wetsuit and accessories for heat insulation, fins for underwater propulsion, a mask (to see underwater), and a snorkel (for moving around the surface of the water without using up breathing gas). Many divers also choose to dive with a diving knife (in case of a tangle with a net, etc.), and night divers may also dive with a waterproof flashlight."],"topics":["diving","hobbies","lifestyle","sports","underwater-diving"]}],"upstream_line":1601915,"upstream_offset":5539988150,"upstream_record_sha256":"7621bea0169bcb12f7a6d8a459886c76511e6bf70067ee95ccd3de5c60d93f6b","word":"scuba set"}
